# Evidence index — 2026-10-17, addenda through 2026-10-26

Single consolidated requirements view: [docs/requirements-evidence-matrix.md](docs/requirements-evidence-matrix.md).

Inspected local retained reports, not a new native execution. Selected file SHA-256
values are in [evidence-hashes.json](docs/handoff/2026-10-17/evidence-hashes.json).
Hashes identify bytes; they do not authenticate outcomes or replace production
seals/protection manifests. Existing attempts remain immutable. This index does
not claim comprehensive historical replay or fresh visual verification.

consumed one-shot attempt and the independent offline rebinding of its retained
bytes (no additional launch).

**The same addendum, fourth entry:** E28 records the operator-authorized
one-shot native run of `D4-CORRIDOR-3` and its close-out. The candidate was

`PARTIAL_EXECUTION_EVIDENCE`, `verified: false`, `status: FAILED_CLEANUP`;
`after-diagnostic.sav` (`b52d0ecf…970b`) is not an authoritative save, no
`after.sav` or `receipt.json` exists, and the read-only replay refuses with

state 5 rather than `TIME_WAIT`, so the workspace and isolated credential were
not disposed). The candidate manifest re-verifies 48/48. No retry, re-sign,
replacement candidate or claim reuse occurred; **Milestone D4 remains
INCOMPLETE (native)** and any future attempt needs a fresh separately
authorized candidate with the `op-03` admission defect corrected.
